&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Real Nilpotent Shears } \mathcal{E}_+(t) = I + t N_+, \; \mathcal{E}_-(s) = I + s N_- \text{ for } t, s > 0 \text{ in } \mathfrak{TP}_{4\text{–}5}\Big) \\[6pt]
&\mathbf{2.\; Total \; Positivity \; (\mathrm{TP}_2) \; in \; \mathrm{Gr}_{\ge 0}(k, n) \; (\mathfrak{TP}_{4\text{–}5}):} \\
&\qquad \mathcal{G}_+(t, s) = \begin{pmatrix} 1+ts & t \\ s & 1 \end{pmatrix} \in \mathrm{TP}_2 \;\; (\det \mathcal{G}_+ = 1 > 0), \qquad \widetilde{P}_+(t, s)^2 = (1 + ts)\,\widetilde{P}_+(t, s) \\[6pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad \boldsymbol{\Big\downarrow} \quad \Big(\text{Two Colliding Moment-Curve Rays } Z(t_a) = (1, t_a, t_a^2, t_a^3) \text{ for } t_0 < t_1 < t_2 < t_3 \text{ in } \mathfrak{TP}_{6\text{–}7}\Big) \\[6pt]
&\mathbf{3.\; Strict \; Positivity \; of \; the \; Amplituhedron \; Foam \; (\mathfrak{TP}_{6\text{–}7}):} \\
&\qquad \big\{\Phi_{01}, \, \Phi_{23}\big\} \;=\; \langle Z(t_0) \, Z(t_1) \, Z(t_2) \, Z(t_3) \rangle\,I_2 \;=\; \prod_{0 \le a < b \le 3} (t_b - t_a)\,I_2 \;>\; 0!

&\qquad Q_+ := N_+ N_- \in P B P \quad (\text{Inside Round-Trip}), \qquad Q_- := N_- N_+ \in (1-P)B(1-P) \quad (\text{Outside Round-Trip}) \\[8pt]
&\begin{array}{c|c|c}
\mathbf{Order} & \mathbf{Gravitational \; Face \; (\text{Sum } +)} & \mathbf{Quantum \; Face \; (\text{Difference } -)} \\ \hline
\rule{0pt}{14pt}\mathbf{1st \; Order} & \Phi = N_+ + N_- = [P, [P, X]] \;\; (\text{Vielbein}) & J = N_+ - N_- = [P, X] \;\; (\text{Phase Current}) \\
\mathbf{2nd \; Order} & V = Q_+ + Q_- = \Phi^2 \;\; (\text{Spacetime Metric}) & \Sigma = Q_+ - Q_- = J\Phi \;\; (\text{Spin Curvature}) \\
\mathbf{Inside \; P} & P\Phi = N_+, \quad PV = Q_+ & PJ = +P\Phi, \quad P\Sigma = +PV \;\; (\mathbf{\text{Identical!}}) \\
\mathbf{Outside \; 1-P} & (1-P)\Phi = N_-, \quad (1-P)V = Q_- & (1-P)J = -(1-P)\Phi, \quad (1-P)\Sigma = -(1-P)V \\
\mathbf{Mutual \; Square} & \Phi^2 = +V, \quad V^2 = +\Sigma^2 & J^2 = -V, \quad \Sigma^2 = +V^2 \;\; (\mathbf{\text{Same Square!}}) \\
\mathbf{Dynamics} & -[\Phi, [\Phi, J]] = -4VJ, \quad V[J, [J, \Phi]] = -4V^2\Phi & 2[\Sigma, \Phi] = +4VJ, \quad [\Sigma, [\Sigma, \Phi]] = +4V^2\Phi
\end{array} \\[8pt]
&\qquad \boldsymbol{\Longrightarrow} \quad \mathbf{Gravity \; = \; S_P \cdot Quantum, \qquad Quantum \; = \; S_P \cdot Gravity \qquad (S_P = 2P - 1, \;\; S_P^2 = 1)!}
\end{aligned}}$$
